# swarm-005940-77tu: merge check before main

Merged on 2026-10-26, about 09:00 UTC, by the chair, at the owner's standing instruction for this run: "Once built, automatically merge to main and push to remote".
- `swarm/swarm-005940-77tu/integration`, tip 34c34a71, holding 73 authored commits and 39 merges, each authored commit with a passing review by a seat other than its author;
- merged onto main f6a9eabb as 396e4c79, then pushed.

Hub 7717 was not restarted, and `dist/` in the main checkout was not rebuilt, so 7717 and launches from the main checkout keep running f6a9eabb until the next build and restart. The room ran on a private hub, :7760, started by the chair from f6a9eabb's build with every seat and recruit on Opus 5.5 at xhigh. The room's own record follows this section unchanged.

## Checks on the merged tree

- The merged tree is identical to 34c34a71 (`git diff` is empty).
- The verifier's verify/final at 34c34a71, in a fresh shared clone:
  - build 0;
  - scripts tsc 0;
  - SMOKE OK;
  - DEPLOY CHECK OK on a 03:12 copy of MAIN/data;
  - the 18 regressions the run added fail at f6a9eabb and pass at 34c34a71.
- The full suite passed 346 of 346 on its third run. Runs 1 and 2 each failed one subtest whose code is unchanged since f6a9eabb: codex-steering's heartbeat race, then bench-rq1's 500 ms deadline kill. Each passes alone. This is the load flakiness already seen on main.
- The chair's own checks, in a detached worktree at 34c34a71 with main's dist left alone: `npm run build` exit 0, `tsc -p tsconfig.scripts.json --noEmit` exit 0, and `PORT=7895 scripts/smoke.ts` SMOKE OK.

## Decision record

This run's launcher predates the room's own fix (80da69ea), so it filed only the first Target, as `reaping-liveness.md`, holding the whole record. The chair then ran `scripts/decision-record.ts swarms/swarm-005940-77tu/result.json` and hit a parse bug in it. A `Slug:` in the prose of Target 6 ("the first Slug: match") was taken as a new Target, so it produced 17 files, including a bogus `match.md` and a 4-line `decision-record.md`.
The chair filed into docs/decisions/proposed/, uncommitted, for the owner to promote with `guard-decisions add`:
- the whole record as `swarm-005940-77tu-rsi-5.md`, also copied to swarms/swarm-005940-77tu/decision-record.md;
- the 16 per-Target files, with Target 6 rebuilt from the split.
The parse bug is open: a `Slug:` must start a line.

## The chair's lapse

The chair stopped watching at 01:45 UTC and was reaped as idle at 02:15 by hub 7760, which still runs f6a9eabb, so the run's own chair_kept fix was not live there.
- The room recorded this in needs/chair-final-merge and concluded without the chair at 03:41.
- Two probes waited on a chair's ok that never came: recruit-settings-1 (later run, see row 16) and machine-load's (c).
- The room's own figure: the chair accounts for 11 of the 15 reaps of a live seat across the records. This lapse is one more case for row 1.

## Still open

From the room's C and NOT SHOWN lines:
- grouping at init is a do-not-build;
- the coverage ledger's live behaviour after af792f45;
- the research sweep and recruit-timing guidance are unmeasured with agents;
- the silence-nudge wakes (idea/silence-nudge-wakes);
- suite timing flakes (idea/suite-timing-flakes);
- the decision-record `Slug:` parse bug above.

# swarm-005940-77tu

**Task:** GOAL: make rooms on this hub organise themselves better: break out bottlenecks, keep working seats alive, choose their shape and size, and use recruits well. Prove each improvement with a benchmark. This is a self-improvement room: the tool you improve is the one you are running on.

WHAT THE OWNER ASKED FOR, in his words, after an 8-hour room on his research server (swarm-164210-9f66, 25-26 Oct):
- "The chair, under my guidance recruited breakouts; yet under the ~3k chat-long thread, there were numerous bottlenecks that never got broken out for."
- "The agents in the room waited a long time for tests (towards the end) and therefore, they automatically got reaped - we want to keep reaping for dead agents, but only truly when needed."
- "Better guidance on when to use a flat room versus grouped with potential automatic assignments when the root/chair initializes the room."
- "better guidance on how many agents to put into a room"
- "general better guidance to verifier, and agents within said room"
- "Potential misc issues such as redundant tool calls, etc."
- "Any new telemetry?"
- "When more aptly to use recruits"
- "There's a whole list of potential problems with the swarm (and others) and I believe we've got enough telemetry to now do another RSI."

HOW THIS ROOM IS SET UP, and why.
- You start small on purpose: four workers and a verifier, in one flat room. The owner wants to see the room recruit and break out when its work calls for it. When a thread bottlenecks, or an item is bigger than one seat, open a break-out (join_room with parent=<this room>) or recruit. Record each time you did, and each time you should have and didn't (friction/missed-breakout-<slug>). That record is evidence for the owner's first ask.
- Every agent is Claude Opus 5.5 at xhigh effort. That includes recruits: pass agent="claude" and model="claude-opus-5-5"; this room's hub gives recruits xhigh. Give a recruit that builds can_edit=true and a brief that says what done looks like.
- The owner is offline overnight. The chair answers for him within this brief and records rulings as decision/chair-<slug>. Anything only the owner can decide goes to needs/<slug> for the morning; don't let it block other work.
- When the room concludes, the chair merges the verifier's integration branch into main and pushes it, without the owner reviewing first. Everything on the integration branch has to be ready for that.

WHERE TO START. Gathering the evidence is your first job. Split it between seats, read each source once, and put what you find on the board. The sources are on your board as resource/*. Start with resource/server-chair-notes: the chair of the 8-hour room wrote down seven hub problems it saw, with message numbers. Its "ideas to fix it" are leads to research, not designs to build. The chair's own quick counts follow. They are leads to check, not findings.
- swarm-164210-9f66 ran from 16:42 UTC on 25 Oct to 00:51 UTC on 26 Oct, and $340 is recorded.
  - The main room has 3,023 messages. Breakouts bk1-bk5 have 105-233 each.
  - Its main room log holds 7,511 attention events, 6,034 call completions, 1,602 board_manifest events, 235 steer_injected, 225 refusals and 88 job_end.
  - Two seats left with "MCP session closed without leave_room (idle 30 min, reaped)" in the main room alone.
- Earlier rounds left these, unfixed:
  - Two rounds in a row (RSI-3 and RSI-4) lost their decision record. The launcher writes it from the verifier's last message only, and only when it finds a `Slug:` line.
  - In RSI-4 the hub's "this room has gone quiet" notice repeated every 5 minutes after seats had named the job they were waiting on. Every repeat woke every seat.
  - In RSI-3 the chair was reaped after 30 idle minutes, like the server chair in note 2.
- Other runs to compare shapes and sizes are listed in resource/server-runs and resource/room-records, flat and grouped, small and large.

HOW TO WORK.
- Research before building, on every item: arXiv first, then primary sources. Put each source you actually read on the board as sources/<id>, with what it shows and whether your idea agrees with it, contradicts it or repeats it.
- Find the cause in the records and the code before you design. A result that says "do not build this, and here is why" counts.
- Each seat claims a different item (claim/<area>, take/<what>). Someone other than the author attacks each idea, and one ranked list stays on the board.
- Be proactive. When you see a flaw, a missing step or a question the owner would ask, raise it, research it and act. Write down each time you had to be told something you could have seen (friction/told-<slug>).
- Guidance counts as a change. The owner asked for guidance on flat versus grouped, room size, recruits, and verifier and seat conduct. It lives in skills/swarm/SKILL.md and prompts/*.md, and it is reviewed and benchmarked like code. Ground it in what the records show: say which runs, n, and where the comparison is confounded.
- Call wait_for_messages with hold_until_actionable=true. Keep your context small, don't paste large outputs into chat, and measure this room with the same tools you improve.

THE RESEARCH SERVER (ssh research-swarm) is for reading only.
- It runs live trading, recorders and a latency-sensitive forward test from 18:56 to 23:00 UTC daily.
- Copy what you need once, to /Users/benji/.frink/builds/room-rsi-5/server-copy/, with `nice -n 19` on the server side. Post it as resource/server-copy and read locally from then on.
- Never write outside /tmp there. Never stop, restart or signal anything there, including its hub (systemctl --user ... chatroom-hub) and cswap.
- Never touch the pmgw user, /opt/pm-gateway, /srv/pm-gateway*, the tmux sessions pm-gateway and pmgw-fast, ~/jq_fwd, the jqf-* timers, ~/.polymarket-test or any key.
- Never run cswap there, and never copy logins between machines.

BENCHMARKS. A change to how rooms behave or what they cost counts as an improvement only with a before and after on the same brief, or a replay over recorded rooms:
- cost per unit of useful work, from runs-table and wait-cost;
- quality on a small fixed benchmark that you choose or build and then keep.
Otherwise call it unmeasured. Say n for every number and where a comparison is confounded.

PROBE ROOMS. You may run small dedicated rooms to test a change, and you are encouraged to.
- Each probe runs on a private hub, with at most 4 seats on claude-opus-5-5 and at most 45 minutes.
- No more than 10 probes in the whole room. Write each probe's cost and outcome to probe/<id> before starting the next.
- Before spending a probe, show its quality measure can tell a good run from a bad one. Two earlier A/Bs scored both arms 1.0 and showed nothing.

THIS ROOM'S OWN RULES.
1. Make exactly one proposal: the final one. Record designs and decisions as decision/<slug> on the board.
2. Review every commit. Someone other than its author writes review/<short-sha>, whose first line is the verify JSON head with "commit" set to that commit and "proposal":"pending". Under it go what they exercised and what was not shown. Reviewers run the suites the commit reaches (scripts/review-suites.ts) and the scripts type-check. The verifier merges only reviewed commits.
3. Acknowledge every inbox/* note addressed to this room by writing <key>.ack.
4. Show what you have taken: claim/<area> for an area, take/<what> for a smaller piece.
5. Every change adds the telemetry that would show it working in a real room.

BUILDING. On your own branch in your own worktree, based on main f6a9eabb:
- a regression test first, shown failing before the change;
- then the change;
- npm run build;
- PORT=<your port> npx tsx scripts/smoke.ts must print SMOKE OK;
- then commit.
Seat commits run the repository's deterministic checks; its AI reviewer checks are off. You may commit with --no-verify when the only failures are pre-existing, and you name them in the message. A change to how agents behave is done only when someone other than its author has watched agents use it, on a private hub built from that branch or in a probe room.

RULES.
- This room runs on its own hub, port 7760. Never stop it, restart it or change its policy.
- Hub 7717 belongs to other sessions. Read its records in MAIN/data, but never stop, restart or call it.
- Leave hub 7750, and whatever runs on port 7719, alone.
- Never build, edit or run a git command that changes anything in MAIN (/Users/benji/Desktop/Personal and learning/agent-chatroom-mcp). Work only in your own worktree. Read MAIN's data/, swarms/ and logs/ there.
- To test a change, start a private hub from your worktree's build with its own data directory (CHATROOM_DATA_DIR=/tmp/hub-<port>), CHATROOM_RECRUIT_AGENT=claude, CHATROOM_RECRUIT_MODEL=claude-opus-5-5 and CLAUDE_CODE_EFFORT_LEVEL=xhigh. Stop it afterwards by its PID (lsof -ti:<port> -sTCP:LISTEN, then kill that PID).
- Ports: seat N uses 78N0 to 78N9 (claude-opus-5-5-3 uses 7830 to 7839), the verifier 7880 to 7889, and recruits and probes 7900 to 7999, after checking the port is free.
- Never use pkill or killall. Stop only processes you started, by PID.
- Polymarket project folders, including /Users/benji/.frink/builds/i-ve-signed-up-to-https-polycopy-app-fol/, are read-only for you. No keys, wallets or orders.
- No process you start may hold more than 2 GB of memory. Read large files as a stream.
- No push, no merge into main, and never run git config. The chair does the merge and push at the end.

TIME. The ceiling is 6 hours, until 06:59 UTC on 2026-10-26 (check the clock with `date -u`). The owner expects four to six hours of work. Conclude earlier only when nothing worth adding remains, and say in the final proposal what you checked to establish that.

FINISH.
- One integration branch, built by the verifier in a worktree and based on main f6a9eabb, holding every reviewed change. At its final tip it must pass:
  - npm run build;
  - tsc -p tsconfig.scripts.json;
  - SMOKE OK;
  - the full suite once;
  - scripts/deploy-check.ts on a copy of hub 7717's data (MAIN/data is read-only: copy it first).
  List every commit that skipped hooks, and why.
- A final proposal that ranks what was built by how much it improves results or cuts cost per unit of useful work. For each item give:
  - the evidence behind it and the sources read;
  - branch, commit and review key;
  - the dev or probe room where agents used it;
  - the telemetry it added;
  - the measured effect with n, or "unmeasured".
- In the same proposal:
  - the guidance written, for flat versus grouped, room size, recruits and break-outs, and verifier and seat conduct;
  - what remains: item, evidence, how to build it.
- The verifier's FINAL message carries the DECISION RECORD. It has one Target per item, each with its own line `Slug: <kebab-case-slug>`. The launcher writes the record from that message only. Send no message after it.

**Done when:** The room has adopted one proposal, made when nothing worth adding remains: a ranked list of what was built (evidence, sources read, branch, commit, review key by a non-author, the dev or probe room where agents used it, telemetry added, and the measured effect with n or 'unmeasured'), the guidance written for flat versus grouped rooms, room size, recruits and break-outs, and verifier and seat conduct, and what remains. Every merged commit has a review entry by someone other than its author. The verifier's integration branch, based on main f6a9eabb, builds, passes the scripts type-check, prints SMOKE OK, passes the full suite once and passes scripts/deploy-check.ts on a copy of hub 7717's data, so the chair can merge it into main and push it unattended.

## Final answer (concluded)

THE RSI-5 ROOM CONCLUDES: keep integration 34c34a71 (branch swarm/swarm-005940-77tu/integration, frozen #1523) = 0f13d23b + af792f45, c11debb2 and fff88fbe (coverage fixes found by probe/coverage-1; review/af792f45, review/c11debb2, review/fff88fbe) + 9b916e10 (npm test runs 2 more regressions, review/9b916e10). At 34c34a71: build 0, scripts tsc 0 (#1523). In verify/final-integration (claude-opus-5-5-1), the 13 in-process regressions this run added fail 13 of 13 at f6a9eabb and pass 13 of 13 at 34c34a71. Verifier #1299: offline suite OK, 344 commands, at 7783b58e (same dist JS); at 0f13d23b build 0, scripts tsc 0, SMOKE OK, DEPLOY CHECK OK (319 logs, 3 boots). Full suite at 34c34a71, run 1 (03:09, 13 min): 345 of 346 commands passed. The one failure was 1 subtest of codex-steering.test.ts, a heartbeat timing race in src/codex/live.ts. Run 2 (03:21, 11 min) also passed 345 of 346: codex-steering passed, and the failure was bench-rq1.test.ts's 500 ms deadline-kill subtest, because the stub had not streamed its init before the kill. Neither test, nor the code it tests, changed since f6a9eabb: the codex diff is empty. Two of bench-rq1's imports changed: src/env.ts only gained exports, and src/result.ts only gained report fields and functions. seatChildEnv, parseClaudeCliOutput and rollupUsage are unchanged (git diff, read by claude-opus-5-5-1). Each test passed when run alone: codex-steering 6/6 by the verifier and 5 of 5 runs by claude-opus-5-5-1, and bench-rq1 4/4 by the verifier. RUN 3 PASSED 346 OF 346 ("[offline] OK (346 commands)"), followed by SMOKE OK and DEPLOY CHECK OK (319 logs, 3 boots, 0 files changed), all at 34c34a71 (#1643, integration/ledger/2/2). Runs 1 and 2 each failed on one timing flake (idea/suite-timing-flakes). It answers the owner as in A, ranks the changes in B, and gives each row's keys, commits and sources in D. A.3-A.5 and A.8 rest on the guidance break-out's conclusion, which passed 4/4 (inbox/swarm-005940-77tu-guidance/conclusion).

A. ANSWERS TO THE OWNER
1. Bottlenecks never broken out.
   - Split on structure, not load: at 552-792 msg/h no seat had 2+ askers waiting over 10 min (evidence/thread-load-0116).
   - 16 of 20 large rooms never broke out. Of 65 worker-opened break-outs, 0 were by Claude workers (60 OpenRouter, 5 codex), and the 11 all-Claude large rooms had 0 worker opens (recount #1184). SKILL.md now says when (9534bff5).
   - An idea with no free seat gets an owed ask to a busy seat or the chair (row 3).
   - A break-out outlives its parent's conclusion. Its conclusion lands on the parent's board, marked late, for 3 h; after that the drop is said and logged. Closing a parent closes its open break-outs, and room_status says where each break-out's conclusion goes (row 8).
   - A verified break-out keeps its verification at the parent (row 7).
   - Owner-7, the unused break-out: it idled 4.5 min until seeded. The owner's message landed 23 s before the seed, so the seed's own effect is UNMEASURED (n=1). loop.md asks for a wip/ key and each seat's first task.
2. Reaped while waiting for tests. A seat is spared while its stream, heartbeat or process lives. Past 120 min only a job or a heartbeat keeps it. The room gets one line naming what keeps a seat. A chair keeps its seat: 2 of 9f66's 4 reaps were the chair's own, and this room's chair was reaped at 02:15 by hub 7760, which still runs f6a9eabb (handoff/reaping-liveness; needs/chair-final-merge) (row 1).
3. Flat or grouped. Do not build automatic grouping at init: shape changed the headline answer in 0 of 4 families (confounded). Start flat and break out by item. On fixed briefs the new SKILL.md moved operators from --groups to flat for 6 h research (2/2) and made them say when to propose (0/2 → 2/2) (probe/operator-check).
4. How many agents. Start at 4-5 seats, each with a nameable item, and grow by recruiting one seat per new item. An extra seat adds its own cost and output and no more; idle seats reach 18% at 13+ (n=4 rooms). Above 6 the launcher prints a note, not a cap (row 12).
5. Verifier and seat guidance.
   - The verifier is told what lands in a flat room (155 of 168 runs planned before this room were flat; the old text described leads): a seat's commit, or a break-out's inbox conclusion. It checks the numbers and caveats before they enter the final.
   - It looks for someone else's review/<sha> first, and while it waits it gives one quoted disagree that carries across amends (row 2).
   - Seats are told to look at claim/, take/ and review/ and take an item before building, and to take figures from the cited record. help/ and join-request/ are dropped.
   - verifier.md does this in 572 words, against 574 on main; recruit.md goes from 455 to 403. result.json records prompt digests, and verifier-conduct.ts scores conduct (row 13).
   - The DECISION RECORD files every Target with a Slug (row 6).
6. Redundant calls. A held wait whose only news is board notices now runs on to the connection's hold (up to 4 min) instead of returning at 55 s. In a replay over 4 runs that would remove 870 of 1,678 such wakes (52%, an estimate that assumes 4-min holds). Live, a hub system line still ended each of 3 held waits at 102-200 s (n=3), so the live reduction is UNMEASURED. An amend posts 1 notice, not 2 (row 5). Most "machine stalls" were the hub blocking itself on `git worktree add` (6 of 9 server episodes, 5 of 5 break-outs), which now runs off its loop (row 4).
7. New telemetry: reap_check; carried_disagrees; hands_*; the worktree event and cause "blocked"; call_completion.woke; decisionRecord; breakout_carry and breakout_fate; coverage; prior-art tags; sizing and seat yield; prompt digests; run shape; carried and re-research; reaps.
8. When to recruit. Take an item yourself when you're free, it's a few steps, or it builds on state you hold. Recruit when it is self-contained and no seat is free, or when it needs a fresh or bulky context. Break out only to argue or build together. Of 291 recruits that joined, 89% delivered, but workers recruit unprompted 1% of the time. Context left is not a trigger: B1 is a measured do-not-build (row 11).
Owner-2, research: SKILL.md "Prior art first". 14 of 17 asking briefs searched within 10 min, against 5 of 60 silent ones (6 of 60 with the merged tagger) (row 10).
Owner-3, every problem tackled: items have coverage states (its first live run was wrong on 3 of 3 and is fixed on replay only, row 9), and "done" needs a non-owner review. Before, 87% of 1,289 claims (1,121) were still held when their owner left, and 6% were released (verifier recount #1055; corrected from 72% in 90ebe5a2). One live claim per seat is not shown either way (36% vs 42%, z≈1.6, within chance). A seat stays for follow-ups that touch its finished claim's files or keys; otherwise it leaves and a fresh recruit takes the next item from a handoff/<slug> (quality effect UNMEASURED) (row 9).
Owner-4, re-research: 2.9% of 238 cross-run fetches and 18% of 132 sources/* re-read a listed id (estimates; the list was not logged). The `carried` event now logs it, and repos, packages and proposed/ records reach the list (row 15).
Owner-6, collisions: board collisions are about 0, because ownership is enforced. A same-file detector would be right 10% of the time: do-not-build.

B. RANKED CHANGES (measured before and after with n; a non-author review; merged; agents in a room). MERGED unless marked.
1. reaping-liveness. 15 of 17 reaps took a live seat (11 of the 15 were chairs; workers and recruits 4 of 5). Dev room bt52 spared 2 of 2 real seats. The ceiling with real seats (probe/reap-ceiling-1 at a 240 s ceiling, watched by claude-opus-5-5-1 in review/reap-ceiling-1): a worker running a 287 s python job went 304 s idle and kept its seat. A verifier on a shell loop was reaped at 243 s with past_ceiling, and its next wait rejoined it with no join_room (n=1 each). The 120-min default, a seat kept past the ceiling by heartbeat, and chair_kept are NOT SHOWN with agents (regression only).
2. verifier-hold. 1,416 amends dropped 267 disagrees, and 0 carried; the same seat re-cast 106 of them, the repeat the carry removes (vote-recast.ts over 319 logs, recounted twice). In 9f66 the verifier's waits returned after a median 9.1 s (n=26) while its disagree stood, against 55 s for others (n=81). Two non-author MCP checks held 8 s and 4 s, against 0 s and 0.5 s at base. In the verifier's drive, an amend that kept the quoted clause dropped the disagree at base and kept it at tip; a disagree without the quote field still drops (evidence/verifier-drive). In rooms: item 8 had 0 disagrees. reap-ceiling-1 (the verifier) and coverage-1 (a worker) each cast one quoted disagree, and the amend replaced the clause. Each seat then agreed with 0 re-casts (probe/verifier-hold-1; vote-recast.ts rerun by claude-opus-5-5-1: 2 dropped, 2 agreed after, 0 re-cast). An amend that keeps the clause, the carry itself, and a verifier hold are NOT SHOWN with agents.
3. hands-for-unowned-ideas (10 commits). Replay (reproduced): the ask would have fired at 42 points. With no ask, 4 of those were resolved within 20 min and 32 never were (evidence/hands-replay). probe/hands-3, watched: claimed in 7 s (n=1; a rescue is unmeasured). The item ask was not exercised in coverage-1 (row 9).
4. machine-load (5). request_agent went from 2.9 s to ≤0.33 s. The false MACHINE line appeared in 2 of 2 runs before and 0 of 4 after (stub recruits). A real write recruit (n=1, probe/recruit-settings-1, watched in review/recruit-settings-1): request_agent 19 ms, its worktree made 6 s before it joined, no stall. A large repo with a real recruit is NOT SHOWN.
5. redundant-calls. Replay (reproduced). The verifier drove it over MCP at base and tip (evidence/verifier-drive): a board-only held wait returned at 55 s at base and at the 90 s hold at tip, and a set plus an amend posted 3 notices at base and 2 at tip. Agents (probe/redundant-calls-1; watcher claude-opus-5-5-4): in item 8, coverage-1 and 8b, 11 amends gave 11 notices and 0 twins. Board notices riding the hold, in item 8b (n=3 waits, 1 seat, the verifier): notices arrived 1.9-20 s into each wait, and the waits held on for 199,970, 181,875 and 101,724 ms. Each was ended by a hub system line, not by a notice, and 2 of those lines were the silence nudge (idea/silence-nudge-wakes) (review/redundant-calls-1; claude-opus-5-5-1's own read finds the same 3). A held wait that runs the full 240 s and then returns is NOT SHOWN.
6. decision-record. Replay of 132 runs: 83 to 95 filed. Live in item 8 (n=1): the new launcher found 2 of 2 "Target N / Slug:" entries in the real verifier's final text and wrote decision-record.md. Its scratch project keeps no decision log, so it proposed no files, and report.md says so. Filing into a real docs/decisions/proposed with agents is NOT SHOWN. Non-author watch: review/decision-record-1 (claude-opus-5-5-4) holds. Its record body is byte-identical to the verifier's final text. This run's launcher predates the change; see the command at the end.
7. breakout-verification. 6 of 6 lost before, 0 in the regression. Agents: 2 unverified break-outs (item 8, reap-ceiling-1) carried the "Not verified by the hub" head to the parent, where 3 of the 4 seats read it with board_get (probe/breakout-verified-1; run B re-read by claude-opus-5-5-1, non-author). A verified break-out with agents is NOT SHOWN.
8. breakout-fate (6). n=36 break-outs; silent drops are now logged. Agents: 2 of 2 dev-room break-outs were carried within 1 ms of concluding, and each seat's fate line before and after matched the hub's word for word (probe/breakout-fate-1; run B re-read by claude-opus-5-5-1, non-author). The launcher found both break-outs by parent link, and they reached result.json and report.md, which the kcj5 case had missed. The late, dropped and cascade paths are covered by the regression only.
9. coverage-tracking (10, including the item ask af7ae3e9 + f617ecba). Agents, probe/coverage-1 (n=1, watched by claude-opus-5-5-4): all 3 items were built and reviewed by non-authors, BUT THE LEDGER KEPT ALL 3 "unowned". A recruitment claim names no item, and a handoff naming the items did not count. The hands ask did not fire (one worker held no claim); had it fired, it would have been false. FIX af792f45: a claim also covers item/<slug> when its owner names it in handoff/<area> or decision/<area>. Replayed on coverage-1's log, it gives 3 reviewed, not 3 unowned. A claim with no handoff yet still reads unowned, unless the recruit's brief named the item/* keys (c11debb2). The fixed ledger with agents is NOT SHOWN (replay of 1 log only). MERGED; review/af792f45 holds. The integration branch swarm/swarm-005940-77tu/integration is at 34c34a71. That commit is a fast-forward of main f6a9eabb, so the chair can merge it into main and push it.

**Evidence** (in verify/final; the logs are in /tmp/verifier-77tu):

- **Hooks:** no commit is known to have skipped hooks.

  - The other 3 are seat-branch merges. Two are clean, and cef95552's tree is identical to 0cd698ed.

**Unverified:**

DECISION RECORD (swarm-005940-77tu, RSI-5; integration 34c34a71 on base main f6a9eabb). No source id below appears in the SETTLED AXES generated at launch, so every source is marked NEW (checked against settledAxes(MAIN) at f6a9eabb).

Target 1: An idle seat is reaped only when nothing shows its client is alive
Slug: reaping-liveness
Context: The idle sweep reaped on MCP lastSeen alone. Over all records, 15 of 17 seat-taking reaps in open rooms took a live seat: 11 of those 15 were chairs, and 4 of the 5 worker or recruit reaps were live. On the 9f66 server run, recruit-11 waited on a background job, was reaped, and came back 50 min later, after recruit-10 had redone its finish.
Ruling: Built in f0fd89af, 2d8213d1, 278fe556 and 3d087d00 (claude-opus-5-5-3; reviews review/f0fd89af by the verifier, and review/2d8213d1, review/278fe556 and review/3d087d00 by claude-opus-5-5-1).
- The sweep spares a session while its GET /mcp stream is open, a heartbeat arrived inside the window, or the latest process sample holds its CLI.
- Past 120 min idle, only a running job or a tool call keeps the seat.
- A chair's idle session closes, but the chair keeps its seat.
- The first spare posts one line naming what keeps the seat.
- Telemetry: reap_check {outcome, alive_by, waiting_on, past_ceiling, idle_min}.
Consequences: Peers no longer need a 15-min keep-alive post. A seat stuck in a shell loop stays for at most 120 min, and one with a job for at most 6 h (JOB_MAX_S). Measured:
- the bt52 dev room spared 2 of 2 real seats;
- probe/reap-ceiling-1 (2 real seats on 0f13d23b, 240 s ceiling, n=1, review/reap-ceiling-1 by claude-opus-5-5-1): a worker with a 287 s python job in waiting_on went 304-307 s idle and kept its seat, and the verifier, whose waiting_on was empty, was reaped at 243 s with past_ceiling true.
NOT SHOWN: the 120-min default, a heartbeat-kept seat past the ceiling, and chair_kept.
Tradeoff: A kept chair keeps a disagree it cast on the current version until it re-votes or the text is amended. How often a chair reap used to lift a veto is not counted.
Researched: replay of 332 room logs (scripts/reap-replay.mjs); jobs ran p95 48 min and p99 166 min (n=1628). The verifier recounted the 15 of 17 and its composition.
Rejected: a "wait until T" tool; auto-reconnect; fixing RSI-4's repeating quiet notice (n=1; revisit if room_idle repeats within 10 min in 2+ rooms).
Revisit-when: reap_check shows a seat reaped past_ceiling that then returns with work, or a spared seat sits past 120 min holding a claim others wait on.

Target 2: A quoted disagree stands across amends that keep its clause
Slug: verifier-hold
Context: On 319 logs, 1,416 amends dropped 267 disagrees and carried 0; the same seat re-cast 106 of them. The vote tool already promised a disagree "stands until ... that text is amended". The 9f66 verifier re-cast its hold 4 times. While its disagree stood, its waits returned after a median 9.1 s (n=26), against 55 s for the other seats (n=81).
Ruling: Built in dd4195b8, dea5937a, 4177dd94 and 37cfc35c (claude-opus-5-5-2; review/dd4195b8, review/dea5937a, review/4177dd94 and review/37cfc35c, all by claude-opus-5-5-3).
- vote(disagree) takes an optional quote. An amend keeps that disagree while the quote survives, and it still counts as standing.
- A carried disagree stands only while its author is present.
- Telemetry: the amend event carries carried_disagrees; scripts/vote-recast.ts replays any log.
Consequences: Driven by the verifier on private hubs (evidence/verifier-drive): at base the disagree was dropped by an "pears"→"plums" amend; at 0f13d23b it carried ("cast at v1; its quoted clause is unchanged in v2") and blocked_by named it. A disagree that quotes only inside its reason, without the quote field, still resets. With agents (probe/verifier-hold-1; author-read, 3 rooms on 0f13d23b): 2 of 2 disagrees set quote; the amend replaced the clause, the disagree dropped, and each seat agreed with 0 re-casts. A carry with agents, and a verifier holding on a pending input, are NOT SHOWN.
Tradeoff: A carried disagree whose point was answered elsewhere in the text stands until its author re-votes. For seats still in the room, what blocks does not widen; only the label changes.
Researched: NEW sources/gerrit-config-labels; NEW sources/github-protected-branches; NEW sources/arXiv:2609.14767.
Rejected: a new "hold" vote kind; carrying the verifier's agree across amends.
Revisit-when: vote-recast.ts on new rooms still shows the same seat re-casting disagrees whose quote survived, or a carried disagree blocks a room after its author's point was met.
RE-TARGET proposal-is-a-document: that axis says an amend "resets votes". Since dd4195b8, an amend keeps a disagree whose quoted clause survives, as keptOnAmend already kept quoted agrees. Evidence: 267 dropped disagrees and 106 re-casts in 319 logs.

Target 3: An idea nobody is free to take gets one owed ask
Slug: hands-for-unowned-ideas
Context: The old idea_no_free_seat hint fired 9 times in 5 rooms across all logs, and 0 recruits followed. It showed only on idle waits, only to a busy author, and ended "carry on and ignore this". 73 of 81 idea/* entries were never claimed.
Ruling: Built in 203a9a94..a2339633 (8 commits), plus f26a2ef5, 34d0cb74, d094b19b and 3eb6bc58 (claude-opus-5-5-4; review/203a9a94 and review/a2339633 for the 8-commit range, plus review/f26a2ef5, review/34d0cb74, review/d094b19b and review/3eb6bc58, all by claude-opus-5-5-2).
- After 10 min, with every worker holding a live claim, the idea's author gets one owed "hands" ask: recruit, claim it after the current claim, or drop it.
- The active chair is asked if the author has left.
- Telemetry: hands_asked and hands_resolved {how}, plus a run-metrics line.
- Off with CHATROOM_HANDS_AFTER_MS=0.
Consequences: Replay (reproduced): 42 would-be asks in 332 logs, 4 resolved within 20 min. probe/hands-3 (n=1, watched): the ask was answered with a claim in 7 s. The live rescue rate is UNMEASURED; the planned main-vs-branch A/B was not run.
Tradeoff: One chat line and one reply per idea. Claims are expected to outnumber recruits.
Researched: NEW sources/anthropic-multi-agent-research-system; NEW sources/arXiv:2603.12229; the 332-log replay (scripts/hands-replay.ts).
Rejected: a trigger for "pairs talking at length" (60-95% of agent chat is pair exchanges); auto-spawning recruits without asking.
Revisit-when: the run-metrics "Ideas nobody was free to take" line shows resolved within 20 min at or below the replay's 10% over 2+ runs, or the ask draws complaints of noise.

Target 4: A hub blocked on its own work is not reported as the machine stalling
Slug: machine-load
Context: 6 of 9 server stall episodes and 5 of 5 break-out stalls were the hub's own spawnSync `git worktree add`, at 5% hub CPU and load ≤2.8. The probe reported every process on the machine as stalling.
Ruling: Built in 6acfc33c, 7581dc80, 0300a3a3, 0a669061 and 3837c012 (machine-load; reviews by claude-opus-5-5-1).
- A worker-thread reference clock separates cause "blocked" from cause "machine".
- Worktrees are made with async execFile, one at a time.
- Telemetry: machine_stall.ref_drift_ms and cause, run-metrics hub_blocks, and a worktree event {ms, waited_ms, ok}.
Consequences: request_agent went from 2.9 s to ≤0.33 s (stub recruits, n=2 per build; recounted by the author from saved JSON). The false MACHINE line appeared 2 of 2 times before and 0 of 4 after. A real write recruit (probe/recruit-settings-1, n=1) got its worktree in 22 ms. A large repo with a real recruit is NOT SHOWN.
Tradeoff: Recruits still start 1-1.3 s apart, because git runs one worktree at a time.
Researched: NEW sources/nodejs-dont-block-the-event-loop; NEW sources/jhiccup-readme; NEW sources/arXiv:1511.02960; NEW sources/scikit-learn-parallelism.
Rejected: a default thread cap (OMP/OPENBLAS=1); renicing seats by default; sparse worktrees; a spawn guard during latency windows.
Revisit-when: a room logs machine_stall with cause machine while ref_drift_ms stays small, or idea/seat-job-cpu shows a seat job behind a stall.

Target 5: Board-only notices ride the full hold, and one amend posts one notice
Slug: redundant-calls
Context: A held wait ended at the asked 55 s once any message had arrived, even a board notice. A board amend posted two lines. In 9f66 that was 758 duplicate lines, 25% of 3,023 messages.
Ruling: Built in 947b85ac and d051dacd (redundant-calls; review/947b85ac by claude-opus-5-5-1). 9b916e10 adds board-notice-hold-regression and run-metrics-regression to npm test (review/9b916e10 by claude-opus-5-5-1).
- A batch of board notices alone holds to the connection's hold (up to 240 s).
- Chat, @-asks, owed items and challenges still end a hold as before.
- An amend posts a single `amended "K" (N chars): old → new` notice.
- Telemetry: call_completion.woke and board_notices, and board_edit.notice_seq.
Consequences: Replay over 4 runs, assuming 4-min holds: 870 of 1,678 board-only wakes go (52%; the verifier recounted 870). That is an estimate; the live reduction is UNMEASURED (n=3 waits, below). Driven by the verifier (evidence/verifier-drive):
- base f6a9eabb: a wait returned at 55 s with 11 board notices;
- 0f13d23b: the wait returned at the 90 s hold with 18 board notices;
- one amend gave 3 lines at base and 2 at the tip.
Board notices did not end waits early at base either; the saving is that board-only news no longer ends the wait at 55 s. With agents (probe/redundant-calls-1): one notice per amend is SHOWN, 11 amends and 11 single notices over 3 rooms. Items 8 and coverage-1 had no board-only window (0 of 63 waits). Item 8b (swarm-024434-k9yw, n=3 waits by 1 seat) did: each of the verifier's 3 held waits got the worker's board notice in its first 55 s and returned at 199,970, 181,875 and 101,724 ms. Base would have returned each at 55 s. No wait reached the 240 s hold: the hub's own system lines (the 3-min silence nudge, the stop warning) ended them, so in a quiet room the gain is capped near 3 min. Those waits are tagged woke "chatter", so wait-cost --by-tool still has no "notices" row. Asks still woke at once: a challenge in 9 s, a proposal in 0 ms (redundant-calls' reading of the 8b log). The verifier recounted the 3 held waits with its own reader (recount/waits8b.py): 199,970, 181,875 and 101,724 ms, each with the worker's board notices in its first 55 s.
Tradeoff: A seat hears of a board write, a plain take release or a failing verify up to the hold later (≤4 min).
Researched: NEW sources/arXiv:2502.11435; NEW sources/arXiv:2606.05304; NEW sources/rfc6202-long-polling; NEW sources/kernel-napi.
Rejected: a hub stub for unchanged re-reads (saves context, not requests); re-targeting "never speak past unread"; changing pass; work-ledger polling (idea/work-ledger-repeats, unowned).
Revisit-when: board-only waits still return near 55 s in a launched room (count them with rode.py, since system lines tag them "chatter"), or a seat misses a reviewer or attack ask.

Target 6: Every Target of the verifier's record is filed, from wherever it was written
Slug: decision-record
Context: RSI-3 and RSI-4 both lost their DECISION RECORD. The launcher read only the last-turn text and the first Slug: match.
Ruling: Built in 80da69ea, 561bcc43 and 0d9cc769 (claude-opus-5-5-1; review/80da69ea, review/561bcc43 and review/0d9cc769, by the verifier).
- The launcher searches the final text, then session transcripts, then chat.
- It splits the record by Target headings or Slug paragraphs.
- It writes decision-record.md plus one proposed/<slug>.md per Target.
- The step never throws.
- Telemetry: result.json decisionRecord and a report.md section.
Consequences: Replay over 132 runs: 83 decisions filed under the old rule, 95 under the new, with all 83 old slugs kept. Live in item 8 (n=1): the new launcher found 2 of 2 Target/Slug entries in the real verifier's final text and wrote decision-record.md. RSI-3 files 11, RSI-4 files 1. This run's launcher (f6a9eabb) predates the fix, so this record is filed afterwards with `npx tsx scripts/decision-record.ts swarms/swarm-005940-77tu/result.json --write docs/decisions/proposed`.
Tradeoff: A record written only on the board, never by the verifier, is still not filed (n=1, swarm-205033-6bp8).
Researched: NEW sources/code.claude.com-headless; NEW sources/nygard-adr-2011.
Rejected: reading the board for records (n=1, and it would file 0).
Revisit-when: a report.md says "decision record: none filed" while the verifier's session shows one.

Target 7: A break-out's verification reaches its parent
Slug: breakout-verification
Context: 6 of 6 verified break-outs lost their verification on the way to the parent's inbox (9f66 5/5, kcj5 1/1). The parent verifier reran everything.
Ruling: Built in c2933768 and 4177dd94 (claude-opus-5-5-2; review/c2933768 and review/4177dd94, by claude-opus-5-5-3), plus SKILL.md 5c8a0bf0 (claude-opus-5-5-4; in review/203a9a94's range). The inbox/<breakout>/conclusion head carries the verification line, or "Not verified by the hub: ...".
- verifier.md (6e6c5e67) tells the parent verifier to check that the numbers and caveats were carried and to rerun only what the note left unverified.
- Telemetry: scripts/breakout-carry.ts.
Consequences: The regression shows 0 lost. With agents, probe/breakout-verified-1: 2 unverified break-outs (item 8 and reap-ceiling-1) carried the "Not verified" head, and the seats read it. A parent verifier's rerun cost with the line present is UNMEASURED.
Tradeoff: The head grows by one line.
Researched: the 9f66 bk2/bk4/bk5 reruns; scripts/breakout-carry.ts over all parents (20 notes).
Rejected: a verifier seat per break-out (it would have caught nothing in n=5, and adds a seat and machine load per break-out).
Revisit-when: a parent verifier reruns a break-out whose note says it was verified, or a carried caveat is dropped from the parent's text.

Target 8: A break-out's fate is stated, logged and tied to its parent
Slug: breakout-fate
Context: Over 36 break-outs in 332 logs: 20 concluded before their parent; 15 were closed by hand within about a second when the owner stopped 3 runs; 1 never concluded. A conclusion the parent could no longer take vanished silently. kcj5's unprefixed break-out was missing from result.json.
Ruling: Built in 09371908, c01ae412, fb9db2b6, 362f9147, 3097a86a and 7a937e7f (breakout-fate; reviews by claude-opus-5-5-3, and review/7a937e7f by claude-opus-5-5-4).
- Every carry attempt logs breakout_carry {delivered|late|dropped}, and a dropped one is said in the break-out.
- A parent's close closes its open break-outs and logs parent_closed.
- The launcher finds break-outs by parent link.
- room_status and GET /rooms carry breakout_fate.line.
Consequences: probe/breakout-fate-1 read the fate line and the carry in item 8 and reap-ceiling-1. The launcher logged "breakouts found by parent link, not by name: probe-findings", and result.json lists probe-findings. 2 of 2 dev rooms passed 9/9 checks, with each conclusion carried within 1 ms. The late carry, the dropped carry and the close cascade are regression-only, NOT SHOWN with agents. A conclusion still does not cascade.
Tradeoff: archive-dead now spares a parent whose break-out still has seats.
Researched: NEW sources/arXiv:2609.01870; NEW sources/arXiv:2607.28802; NEW sources/vorpus-structured-concurrency; NEW sources/erlang-otp-supervisor.
Rejected: auto-archiving concluded break-outs (lingering cost $3.29 over n=15); forwarding post-conclusion board writes; auto-prefixing names.
Revisit-when: breakout_carry logs a dropped outcome in a live run, or a stopped run leaves break-outs open.

Target 9: Each brief problem is an item with an owner and a coverage state
Slug: coverage-tracking
Context: 87% of 1,289 claims (1,121) were still held when their owner left, and 6% were released (the verifier recounted 1,121 and corrected the earlier 72% in 90ebe5a2). No board view named the brief's problems. The chair recruited room-size twice.
Ruling: Built in 4ddcfe6d, a5d496a7, eacedc5b, 416bc344, af7ae3e9, f617ecba, 90ebe5a2, af792f45, c11debb2 and fff88fbe (coverage-tracking; reviews by room-size, claude-opus-5-5-4 and redundant-calls; review/af792f45, review/c11debb2 and review/fff88fbe by claude-opus-5-5-4).
- af792f45: a claim also covers the item/* keys its writer or owner names in handoff/<area> or decision/<area>.
- c11debb2: a recruit's claim lists the item/* keys its request_agent brief names. A line that names an item only to say nobody did it ("not done", "nobody", "todo") does not cover it.
- item/<slug> entries, seeded from "## item:" headings via --resources.
- The hub computes the states unowned, owned, reviewed, built, done, orphaned and dropped. "done" needs a non-owner passing review.
- Shown in room_status.coverage and result.json.
- An unowned item with no free seat gets the hands ask.
- Guidance: one live claim per seat; a finished seat stays only for follow-ups touching its files or keys.
- Telemetry: the coverage event and scripts/coverage-replay.ts.
Consequences: With agents (probe/coverage-1, n=1, watched by claude-opus-5-5-4 in review/af7ae3e9): all 3 items were built and reviewed by non-authors, but the ledger kept all 3 "unowned", because claim/build-utils named no item/*. The hands ask was not exercised (0 events), since one worker held no claim. Fixed in af792f45: a Hub replay of coverage-1's real log now gives 3 reviewed against 3 unowned (coverage-tracking's replay-check, not rerun by the verifier). The verifier reran the regressions: 17/17 with the af792f45 test, 1 failing at its parent; 19/19 at c11debb2. probe-undone ("NOT DONE, nobody has it: item/chunk"): chunk went owned, then orphaned, at af792f45; it stays unowned at c11debb2. F2 (review/c11debb2): an item bulleted under a negated header ("NOT DONE:", "FOLLOW-UPS:") still read owned, then orphaned. fff88fbe fixes it: coverage-regression fails 1 of 20 at c11debb2 and passes 20/20 at fff88fbe. KNOWN LIMIT n2 (review/fff88fbe): other negative headers ("Next steps:", "Remaining:", "Blocked:") are not markers, so their bullets still count as covered. How often handoffs take that shape is UNMEASURED. The fixed ledger has not run in a second dev room. One claim per seat is not shown either way (36% vs 42%, z≈1.6). The quality effect of handing follow-ups to fresh recruits is UNMEASURED.
Tradeoff: The chair or launcher must write the items, because parsing the brief picked up rules in this room.
Researched: NEW sources/arXiv:2411.04468; NEW sources/arXiv:2402.18216; NEW sources/chroma-context-rot; NEW sources/anthropic-context-engineering; NEW sources/arXiv:2607.04697; NEW sources/arXiv:2604.03551; NEW sources/brun-fse2011-crystal.
Rejected: a same-file collision detector (86 of 91 branch pairs share a file but 9 conflict, so it would be right 10% of the time); a hub cap on claims per seat; offering the door at once on done.
Revisit-when: coverage-replay shows items orphaned at conclusion in 2+ runs launched with items, "done" items later fail review, or a handoff whose header the guard does not know ("Next steps:") leaves an unbuilt item marked owned.

Target 10: Rooms search for and run prior art, and telemetry shows whether they did
Slug: research-depth
Context: 14 of 17 rooms whose brief asked for research searched within 10 min, against 5 of 60 silent ones (6 of 60 under the merged tagger: swarm-235212-ozkw-room counts at 0.6 min). Outside code was run in 0 of 77 rooms.
Ruling: Built in 024099de, d9dfa21f, 799792c4 and daeb8132 (research-depth; reviews by telemetry-gaps).
- refsIn ids, shared with telemetry-gaps.
- The work ledger tags search, fetch and run.
- run-metrics reports minutesTo firstPriorArt, firstCodeFetch and firstThirdPartyRun, plus refs handed vs found.
- SKILL.md "Prior art first": a pre-launch check, a sweep owner within 15 min, and a safe-run rule with "blocked: <reason>".
Consequences: Before-side only. Pre-registered: the next room launched with the sweep item should search by 15 min and fetch, then run or record a block by 30 min.
Tradeoff: Tags are heuristics on commands and hosts.
Researched: NEW sources/arXiv:2409.07440, 2508.18993, 2505.21577, 2609.02749, 2501.04227, 2406.14497, 2402.17453 and 2603.09290.
Rejected: a hub nudge at T+N (it would fire in 48 of 60 silent rooms, with unknown precision); a persistent scout (RSI-3's A/B saturated, 4/4 vs 4/4, +46% cost).
Revisit-when: two rooms in a row launched with the bullet's sweep item miss a search by 15 min. Then build the T+N nudge and test it in a dev room.

Target 11: When to take an item, recruit or break out
Slug: recruit-timing
Context: Of 291 recruits that joined, 89% delivered. Claude workers recruit unprompted 1% of the time (3/365). Holding a second claim does not slow a first output (5.1 vs 5.4 min). Context size shows no reliable harm to output (65% vs 75%, z=1.37).
Ruling: Built in 1e6ff916, 2e60fcd4, 48244a04, c10e972b and 9534bff5 (recruit-timing, plus claude-opus-5-5-4 for SKILL.md; reviews by breakout-fate and claude-opus-5-5-2).
- Take an item yourself when you are free, it is a few steps, or it builds on state you hold.
- Recruit when it is self-contained and no seat is free, or when it needs a bulky or fresh context.
- Break out to argue or build together, seeded.
- loop.md drops "recruit freely".
- Telemetry: recruit-census --rows, which reads recruit_spawn.
Consequences: Records only. Agents using the new text are NOT SHOWN.
Tradeoff: The rule asks seats to judge "self-contained", which no signal checks.
Researched: NEW sources/arXiv:2512.08296; NEW sources/arXiv:2606.09730; NEW sources/arXiv:2605.02801; NEW sources/kimi-k2.5-agent-swarm; NEW sources/cognition-dont-build-multi-agents; NEW sources/code.claude.com-sub-agents; NEW sources/anthropic-multi-agent-research-system; NEW sources/arXiv:2603.12229.
Rejected: B1, the context-headroom hint (its outcome link does not hold up); auto-spawn; a duplicate-recruit guard (n=2); a depth cap (depth-2 recruits delivered 85%).
Revisit-when: recruit-census rows from runs at this loop.md show recruits delivering below 80%, or workers still recruiting at 1%.

Target 12: Size a room by the items you can name, and grow by recruiting
Slug: room-size
Context: Within a room, an added seat brings one seat's cost and one seat's output, and no more. Idle seats rise from 4% at 2-3 seats to 18% at 13+ (110 rooms: 27 at 2-3 seats, 4 at 13+, recounted by the verifier with size_table.py; confounded by task and era). On a fixed pool, 15 seats landed no more than 3 did.
Ruling: Built in 417aab70, 4bbc6e26 and d3b3868b (room-size; reviews by room-shape).
- Above 6 agents in one room the launcher prints a size note, never a cap.
- result.json carries sizing.
- runs-table shows seat yield {peakSeats, idleSeats, seatsFor80}.
- SKILL.md: start at 4-6 and recruit per item.
Consequences: Operator check: "lots of agents" moved from 8 to 6 (2/2 per arm). This room: 17 seats, 0 idle, $294 in 1.04 h (n=1). The effect on room outcomes is UNMEASURED.
Tradeoff: The note's brief_items reads the brief's first numbered list, which can be its rules.
Researched: NEW sources/arXiv:2512.08296; NEW sources/arXiv:2606.02646; NEW sources/arXiv:2609.31563.
Rejected: a cap or refusal (settled flat-seats-capped-per-model); a hub live-seat warning on recruit; a new --agents default.
Revisit-when: seat yield shows idle seats above 10% in 2+ rooms launched under this guidance, or a same-brief 4 vs 8 run is funded.

Target 13: Verifier and seat prompts carry the rules seats were told by hand
Slug: seat-verifier-guidance
Context: verifier.md described leads and sub-rooms, but 155 of 168 runs planned before this room were flat. Seats were told "look at claim/take/review first" and "figures from the record" in 46 friction entries. filedigest/ was written 0 times in 332 logs.
Ruling: Built in 6e6c5e67, 40026391, ca98f709, 68d9408a and 8eae486e (seat-verifier-guidance; review/6e6c5e67 by claude-opus-5-5-2, review/8eae486e by claude-opus-5-5-4).

- recruit.md goes from 455 to 403 words.
- result.json gets prompt digests.
- scripts/verifier-conduct.ts scores conduct.
Consequences: With agents (probe/svg-1, item 8, n=1, verifier.md 64ab010712ca): the verifier
- wrote a review before any proposal;
- ran median() on its own inputs;
- reran the bench;
- read the break-out's inbox conclusion unprompted;
- filed Slug lines.
The verifier named both planted bugs 9 s after reading the code, so the plant did not discriminate, and the prompt's own effect is UNMEASURED (review/svg-1 by claude-opus-5-5-2). The disagree rules (0 disagrees) and recruit.md (0 recruits) are NOT SHOWN.
Tradeoff: recruit.md no longer spells out the claim JSON fields. Tools and refusals carry them.
Researched: NEW sources/arXiv:2507.11538; NEW sources/arXiv:2402.10962; NEW sources/arXiv:2608.11727; NEW sources/anthropic-context-engineering.
Rejected: editing minimal.md (settled minimal-prompt-hub-carries-coordination); two verifier templates; dropping filedigest/ (left as idea/read-digest-unused).
Revisit-when: over the next 10 flat rooms at this verifier.md digest, a verifier review/verify appears in under 50% of rooms, or a seat chats "still holding" while its disagree stands.

Target 14: Start flat and break out by item; no automatic grouping at init
Slug: room-shape
Context: Shape changed the headline answer in 0 of 4 families that asked one question both ways (confounded). Grouped cost 1.1-6x per useful result. Grouped did conclude long open research 4 of 4 times.
Ruling: Built in 8b73d0ed, 7bd31ac9, ce6347a9, ad996e23 and 548aaa17 (room-shape; reviews by room-size and recruit-timing).

Consequences: Operator check, 24 calls: a 6 h research brief moved from --groups 2/2 to flat 2/2, and "when to propose" went from 0/2 to 2/2. Room outcomes are NOT SHOWN.
Tradeoff: No same-brief flat vs grouped run over hours.
Researched: NEW sources/arXiv:2512.08296; NEW sources/arXiv:2502.02533.
Rejected: automatic grouping at init by launcher, planner or chair.
Revisit-when: a same-brief flat vs grouped run over hours shows grouped landing a better scored answer, or the owner changes the CLI default from planned.

Target 16: A recruit inherits its launching run's seat settings
Slug: recruit-settings

- The spawner overlays them, walking parent links: the run first, then the hub, then the default.
- --effort sets the level.
- Model, accounts and other env are not inherited.
- Telemetry: recruit_spawn with each setting's source.
Consequences: probe/recruit-settings-1 (n=1 per side, watched in review/recruit-settings-1):
- before, at 74fca9be: EFFORT=high, hooks copied, no record;
- after: EFFORT=xhigh, no hooks, source run.
The quality and cost effect of parity is UNMEASURED.

## Groups

### swarm-005940-77tu-guidance (concluded)

GUIDANCE BREAK-OUT CONCLUSION (swarm-005940-77tu-guidance, for the parent's owner asks 1, 3, 4, 5 and 8). Full text with every row: wip/guidance (sections 1, 2, 6) and wip/guidance-2 (sections 3-5, path, commits) on this board. Format: CLAIM | evidence, n | confound | target.

PATH: no fold. Each hunk merges with its own reviewed commit, through the parent's verifier (decision/skill-md-integration). This is the claim list, not a replacement text. Trial merge: 9 of 12 branches clean (evidence/skill-md-trial-merge).

1 BREAK-OUTS (claude-opus-5-5-4)
- Split on structure (≥3 seats writing one artifact), not load | the parent at 552-792 msg/h had no seat with ≥2 askers waiting >10 min (evidence/thread-load-0116, 01:42 re-measure) | n=1 room | SKILL.md
- Claude seats in flat rooms do not open break-outs unprompted, so the hub asks | 16 of 20 large rooms never broke out; all 62 worker opens were OpenRouter on loop.md | runner and prompt are confounded | hub
- The opener seeds first: one wip/<artifact> with an owner per section, plus one message naming each seat's first edit | CONSISTENT WITH seeding, NOT SEPARABLE from the owner's prompt. This room went from 0 member messages and 0 entries in 4.5 min to 4 and 4 in the next 4.5 min, but owner input 7, which asked about this idle break-out, reached every member 23 s before the seed. In kcj5, 3 of 4 break-outs were live within a minute when the opener's own work moved in, but those were codex seats on another runner (ch_a47cc782) | n=1 here, so the seed's own effect is UNMEASURED | SKILL.md (86e3f45d, f26a2ef5)
- Unowned idea while every worker is busy: an owed ask to its author (the hands ask) | before: the passive notice fired 9 times in 332 logs with 0 recruits; the replay gives 42 would-be asks, 4 resolved within 20 min, 32 never; this parent: 6 would-be asks, 0 resolved | after: probe/hands-3 claimed in 7 s and replied in 13 s, n=1, watched by claude-opus-5-5-2 | the live rescue effect is UNMEASURED | hub + SKILL.md (203a9a94..a2339633)

2 RECRUITS (recruit-timing; SKILL.md 9534bff5, loop.md 2e60fcd4)
- Take it yourself when you are free, it is a few steps, or it builds on state you hold | a busy seat's first output 5.1 min vs a recruit's 5.4 (n=125/145) | observational
- Recruit one seat when the item is self-contained AND no seat is free, OR it needs a fresh context, OR its working set is bulky | From recruit-census.py at c10e972b, MAIN/data: of 291 recruits that joined, 89% delivered and 55% were cited, and the in-room median was $2.92 (n=35). 9f66's break-out seats ran a median $10.64 (n=15). SKILL.md 9534bff5 cites the earlier pass: 87% of 193, $3.6 and 11 min in-room (n=38) | board-key proxies
- A seat's own context size is not a reason to hand an item on | 75% vs 65%, z=1.37, n=154 | collinear with seat age
- Workers recruit unprompted 1% of the time (3/365), so the hub asks | era and provider
- Do not recruit over the machine gate, into an area that already has a recruit, or when a free seat can start | n small

3 SHAPE (room-shape; 8b73d0ed, 7bd31ac9, ce6347a9, ad996e23)
- Default flat. Shape changed the headline answer in 0 of 4 families; grouped cost 1.1-6x, though grouped always concluded (4/4 vs 38/49 long flat runs) | no same-brief run over hours
- One codebase: flat. Groups only for ≥3 independent sub-areas under one machine scoring rule, at ~3x the cost | mrw4 74 vs 5y46 35 | n=1 vs 2
- Number the parts before any numbered rules, because seat-targets reads the first list | this room's openings took "rule 2" | n=1
- No automatic grouping at init | seat-targets' downstream effect is UNMEASURED

4 SIZE (room-size; 417aab70, 4bbc6e26, d3b3868b, merged cbc69cd8)
- Start at 4-5 seats; grow by recruiting into a named, unowned item | recruits 2.70 vs launch seats 2.54 findings/seat-h, 37 rooms, p=0.74 | findings, not landed results
- One seat per item you can name. Past 4-5 builders, review sets the pace | Room15 = Room3 on items landed, at 2.5-3.5x $/item | small n
- At 6-8 seats, findings per seat fall (3.2 at 4-5, 2.5 at 6-8; room-size checked the row, #872); above 8, idle seats rise to 10-18%: break out instead | n=110 Claude rooms, rebuilt to the row (d3b3868b; attack/wip/guidance-2) | task and era; a launcher note, not a cap

5 SEAT AND VERIFIER (seat-verifier-guidance; 6e6c5e67, 40026391, ca98f709, 68d9408a)
- verifier.md becomes shape-neutral (572 words); recruit.md goes to 403 words | 139 of 155 verifier runs were flat | the mismatch has no outcome measure
- The parent's verifier checks a break-out's result, since a break-out usually has no verifier; a reviewer is never the author | 9f66: 5 of 5 break-outs had no verifier | n=1 run | SKILL.md 5c8a0bf0
- Look before you build (claim/, take/, review/), write take/ first, and take figures from the cited record | 10 duplicate-work and 9 told incidents | self-reported

6 COLLISIONS AND COVERAGE (coverage-tracking; 4ddcfe6d, a5d496a7, eacedc5b)
- The chair writes item/<slug> per problem; room_status.coverage shows each item's state | room-size was recruited twice, and six asks were found unowned by hand | n=1 room
- One live claim per seat by default | 36% vs 42%, z≈1.6, within chance (74 rooms), so this is NOT evidence that two claims hurt (ch_33ac9778) | the default rests on one owner per item for the coverage ledger, not on yield
- Board-key collisions are near zero (380 refusals, 0 overwrites). The real ones are chat (19% of 9f66's sends were refused as unread) and files (10 authors edited SKILL.md) | n=182 rooms
- "Done" needs a non-author review | 2 of 2 needed rework after review

NOT SHOWN: probe/operator-check (old vs new SKILL.md), probe/svg-1, and a live loop.md room. Shown with agents: the hands ask (probe/hands-3).

Topic: GOAL: make rooms on this hub organise themselves better: break out bottlenecks, keep working seats alive, choose their shape and size, and use recruits well. Prove each improvement with a benchmark. This is a self-improvement room: the tool you improve is the one you are running on.

    - Other runs to compare shapes and sizes are listed in resource/server-runs and resource/room-records, flat and grouped, small and large.
    
    HOW TO WORK.
    - Research before building, on every item: arXiv first, then primary sources. Put each source you actually read on the board as sources/<id>, with what it shows and whether your idea agrees with it, contradicts it or repeats it.
    - Find the cause in the records and the code before you design. A result that says "do not build this, and here is why" counts.
    - Each seat claims a different item (claim/<area>, take/<what>). Someone other than the author attacks each idea, and one ranked list stays on the board.
    - Be proactive. When you see a flaw, a missing step or a question the owner would ask, raise it, research it and act. Write down each time you had to be told something you could have seen (friction/told-<slug>).
    - Guidance counts as a change. The owner asked for guidance on flat versus grouped, room size, recruits, and verifier and seat conduct.     A. ANSWERS TO THE OWNER
    1. Bottlenecks never broken out.
       - Split on structure, not load: at 552-792 msg/h no seat had 2+ askers waiting over 10 min (evidence/thread-load-0116).
       - 16 of 20 large rooms never broke out. Of 65 worker-opened break-outs, 0 were by Claude workers (60 OpenRouter, 5 codex), and the 11 all-Claude large rooms had 0 worker opens (recount #1184). SKILL.md now says when (9534bff5).
       - An idea with no free seat gets an owed ask to a busy seat or the chair (row 3).
       - A break-out outlives its parent's conclusion. Its conclusion lands on the parent's board, marked late, for 3 h; after that the drop is said and logged. Closing a parent closes its open break-outs, and room_status says where each break-out's conclusion goes (row 8).
       - A verified break-out keeps its verification at the parent (row 7).
       - Owner-7, the unused break-out: it idled 4.5 min until seeded. The owner's message landed 23 s before the seed, so the seed's own effect is UNMEASURED (n=1). loop.md asks for a wip/ key and each seat's first task.
    2. Reaped while waiting for tests. A seat is spared while its stream, heartbeat or process lives. Past 120 min only a job or a heartbeat keeps it. The room gets one line naming what keeps a seat. A chair keeps its seat: 2 of 9f66's 4 reaps were the chair's own, and this room's chair was reaped at 02:15 by hub 7760, which still runs f6a9eabb (handoff/reaping-liveness; needs/chair-final-merge) (row 1).
    3. Flat or grouped. Do not build automatic grouping at init: shape changed the headline answer in 0 of 4 families (confounded). Start flat and break out by item. On fixed briefs the new SKILL.md moved operators from --groups to flat for 6 h research (2/2) and made them say when to propose (0/2 → 2/2) (probe/operator-check).
    4. How many agents. Start at 4-5 seats, each with a nameable item, and grow by recruiting one seat per new item. An extra seat adds its own cost and output and no more; idle seats reach 18% at 13+ (n=4 rooms). Above 6 the launcher prints a note, not a cap (row 12).
    5. Verifier and seat guidance.
       - The verifier is told what lands in a flat room (155 of 168 runs planned before this room were flat; the old text described leads): a seat's commit, or a break-out's inbox conclusion. It checks the numbers and caveats before they enter the final.
       - It looks for someone else's review/<sha> first, and while it waits it gives one quoted disagree that carries across amends (row 2).
       - Seats are told to look at claim/, take/ and review/ and take an item before building, and to take figures from the cited record. help/ and join-request/ are dropped.
       - verifier.md does this in 572 words, against 574 on main; recruit.md goes from 455 to 403. result.json records prompt digests, and verifier-conduct.ts scores conduct (row 13).
       - The DECISION RECORD files every Target with a Slug (row 6).
    6. Redundant calls. A held wait whose only news is board notices now runs on to the connection's hold (up to 4 min) instead of returning at 55 s. In a replay over 4 runs that would remove 870 of 1,678 such wakes (52%, an estimate that assumes 4-min holds). Live, a hub system line still ended each of 3 held waits at 102-200 s (n=3), so the live reduction is UNMEASURED. An amend posts 1 notice, not 2 (row 5). Most "machine stalls" were the hub blocking itself on `git worktree add` (6 of 9 server episodes, 5 of 5 break-outs), which now runs off its loop (row 4).
    7. New telemetry: reap_check; carried_disagrees; hands_*; the worktree event and cause "blocked"; call_completion.woke; decisionRecord; breakout_carry and breakout_fate; coverage; prior-art tags; sizing and seat yield; prompt digests; run shape; carried and re-research; reaps.
    8. When to recruit. Take an item yourself when you're free, it's a few steps, or it builds on state you hold. Recruit when it is self-contained and no seat is free, or when it needs a fresh or bulky context. Break out only to argue or build together. Of 291 recruits that joined, 89% delivered, but workers recruit unprompted 1% of the time. Context left is not a trigger: B1 is a measured do-not-build (row 11).
    Owner-2, research: SKILL.md "Prior art first". 14 of 17 asking briefs searched within 10 min, against 5 of 60 silent ones (6 of 60 with the merged tagger) (row 10).
    Owner-3, every problem tackled: items have coverage states (its first live run was wrong on 3 of 3 and is fixed on replay only, row 9), and "done" needs a non-owner review. Before, 87% of 1,289 claims (1,121) were still held when their owner left, and 6% were released (verifier recount #1055; corrected from 72% in 90ebe5a2). One live claim per seat is not shown either way (36% vs 42%, z≈1.6, within chance). A seat stays for follow-ups that touch its finished claim's files or keys; otherwise it leaves and a fresh recruit takes the next item from a handoff/<slug> (quality effect UNMEASURED) (row 9).
    Owner-4, re-research: 2.9% of 238 cross-run fetches and 18% of 132 sources/* re-read a listed id (estimates; the list was not logged). The `carried` event now logs it, and repos, packages and proposed/ records reach the list (row 15).
    Owner-6, collisions: board collisions are about 0, because ownership is enforced. A same-file detector would be right 10% of the time: do-not-build.
    
    B. RANKED CHANGES (measured before and after with n; a non-author review; merged; agents in a room). MERGED unless marked.
    1. reaping-liveness. 15 of 17 reaps took a live seat (11 of the 15 were chairs; workers and recruits 4 of 5). Dev room bt52 spared 2 of 2 real seats. The ceiling with real seats (probe/reap-ceiling-1 at a 240 s ceiling, watched by claude-opus-5-5-1 in review/reap-ceiling-1): a worker running a 287 s python job went 304 s idle and kept its seat. A verifier on a shell loop was reaped at 243 s with past_ceiling, and its next wait rejoined it with no join_room (n=1 each). The 120-min default, a seat kept past the ceiling by heartbeat, and chair_kept are NOT SHOWN with agents (regression only).
    2. verifier-hold. 1,416 amends dropped 267 disagrees, and 0 carried; the same seat re-cast 106 of them, the repeat the carry removes (vote-recast.ts over 319 logs, recounted twice). In 9f66 the verifier's waits returned after a median 9.1 s (n=26) while its disagree stood, against 55 s for others (n=81). Two non-author MCP checks held 8 s and 4 s, against 0 s and 0.5 s at base. In the verifier's drive, an amend that kept the quoted clause dropped the disagree at base and kept it at tip; a disagree without the quote field still drops (evidence/verifier-drive). In rooms: item 8 had 0 disagrees. reap-ceiling-1 (the verifier) and coverage-1 (a worker) each cast one quoted disagree, and the amend replaced the clause. Each seat then agreed with 0 re-casts (probe/verifier-hold-1; vote-recast.ts rerun by claude-opus-5-5-1: 2 dropped, 2 agreed after, 0 re-cast). An amend that keeps the clause, the carry itself, and a verifier hold are NOT SHOWN with agents.
    3. hands-for-unowned-ideas (10 commits). Replay (reproduced): the ask would have fired at 42 points. With no ask, 4 of those were resolved within 20 min and 32 never were (evidence/hands-replay). probe/hands-3, watched: claimed in 7 s (n=1; a rescue is unmeasured). The item ask was not exercised in coverage-1 (row 9).
    4. machine-load (5). request_agent went from 2.9 s to ≤0.33 s. The false MACHINE line appeared in 2 of 2 runs before and 0 of 4 after (stub recruits). A real write recruit (n=1, probe/recruit-settings-1, watched in review/recruit-settings-1): request_agent 19 ms, its worktree made 6 s before it joined, no stall. A large repo with a real recruit is NOT SHOWN.
    5. redundant-calls. Replay (reproduced). The verifier drove it over MCP at base and tip (evidence/verifier-drive): a board-only held wait returned at 55 s at base and at the 90 s hold at tip, and a set plus an amend posted 3 notices at base and 2 at tip. Agents (probe/redundant-calls-1; watcher claude-opus-5-5-4): in item 8, coverage-1 and 8b, 11 amends gave 11 notices and 0 twins. Board notices riding the hold, in item 8b (n=3 waits, 1 seat, the verifier): notices arrived 1.9-20 s into each wait, and the waits held on for 199,970, 181,875 and 101,724 ms. Each was ended by a hub system line, not by a notice, and 2 of those lines were the silence nudge (idea/silence-nudge-wakes) (review/redundant-calls-1; claude-opus-5-5-1's own read finds the same 3). A held wait that runs the full 240 s and then returns is NOT SHOWN.
    6. decision-record. Replay of 132 runs: 83 to 95 filed. Live in item 8 (n=1): the new launcher found 2 of 2 "Target N / Slug:" entries in the real verifier's final text and wrote decision-record.md. Its scratch project keeps no decision log, so it proposed no files, and report.md says so. Filing into a real docs/decisions/proposed with agents is NOT SHOWN. Non-author watch: review/decision-record-1 (claude-opus-5-5-4) holds. Its record body is byte-identical to the verifier's final text. This run's launcher predates the change; see the command at the end.
    7. breakout-verification. 6 of 6 lost before, 0 in the regression. Agents: 2 unverified break-outs (item 8, reap-ceiling-1) carried the "Not verified by the hub" head to the parent, where 3 of the 4 seats read it with board_get (probe/breakout-verified-1; run B re-read by claude-opus-5-5-1, non-author). A verified break-out with agents is NOT SHOWN.
    8. breakout-fate (6). n=36 break-outs; silent drops are now logged. Agents: 2 of 2 dev-room break-outs were carried within 1 ms of concluding, and each seat's fate line before and after matched the hub's word for word (probe/breakout-fate-1; run B re-read by claude-opus-5-5-1, non-author). The launcher found both break-outs by parent link, and they reached result.json and report.md, which the kcj5 case had missed. The late, dropped and cascade paths are covered by the regression only.
    9. coverage-tracking (10, including the item ask af7ae3e9 + f617ecba). Agents, probe/coverage-1 (n=1, watched by claude-opus-5-5-4): all 3 items were built and reviewed by non-authors, BUT THE LEDGER KEPT ALL 3 "unowned". A recruitment claim names no item, and a handoff naming the items did not count. The hands ask did not fire (one worker held no claim); had it fired, it would have been false. FIX af792f45: a claim also covers item/<slug> when its owner names it in handoff/<area> or decision/<area>. Replayed on coverage-1's log, it gives 3 reviewed, not 3 unowned. A claim with no handoff yet still reads unowned, unless the recruit's brief named the item/* keys (c11debb2). The fixed ledger with agents is NOT SHOWN (replay of 1 log only). MERGED; review/af792f45 holds. Its F1 (a line saying an item is NOT done still marked it owned) is fixed in c11debb2, which also lists the item/* keys a recruit's brief names in its claim (review/c11debb2, MERGED). Its F2, a "NOT DONE:" or "FOLLOW-UPS:" header with the item in a bullet below, is fixed in fff88fbe (review/fff88fbe), MERGED in 34c34a71. KNOWN LIMITS: other negative headers such as "Next steps:" (handoff/coverage-tracking), and item names without the item/ prefix.

\#29 [2026-10-26T01:01:45.958Z] claude-opus-5-5-1 (chat): Good, please do drive it. Causes found so far, both from the records. RSI-4 (073112): your 3,641-char record at 09:44:31 was in the CLI's earlier turn. A background watcher woke you at 09:51 and your 309-char reply became the `result` text, so src/swarm.ts:916 found no DECISION RECORD. The record survives only in the session jsonl (scorecard.transcript). RSI-3 (092526): the record was there but written "**Target 1: claude-seats-compact-at-300k.**" with no `Slug:` line, so the regex at :919 matched 0. It also writes only the first slug even when there are several Targets. Fix plan: look through final text, then the session transcript, then your room messages for the newest DECISION RECORD, write one file per Target, and add result.decisionRecord {source, slugs, missing} plus a report line. The regression test replays both runs' real text.
\#30 [2026-10-26T01:03:05.694Z] claude-opus-5-5-1 (chat): @verifier @chair-claude this matters for THIS room's record. The launcher running us is main f6a9eabb, so my fix will not apply to it. The Claude Code headless docs (code.claude.com/docs/en/headless, "Background tasks at exit") say that if a background command or Monitor is still running after your last turn, Claude "takes one more turn with the outcome", and json output keeps only that last turn's `result`. That is exactly how RSI-4 lost its record. So, verifier: (1) stop every background job and Monitor of yours BEFORE your final reply; (2) put the DECISION RECORD in your final reply text itself, not only in a room message; (3) old code files only the FIRST `Slug:` (src/swarm.ts:919). The other Targets survive only in result.json verifier.output. I'm adding a re-extract command so the chair can file every Target from result.json after the run.

Before my final reply I'll confirm that no background job or Monitor of mine is still running. The record goes in that reply's text, with the Target I most need filed listed first. \#254 [2026-10-26T01:23:31.408Z] recruit-timing (chat): @recruit-settings, per telemetry-gaps' #241, here are the state fields for recruit_spawn, all cheap at the spawn site.
    - kind: in-room / breakout (new_room) / replacement (replacing).
    - by_role: the requester's role.
    - workers: active non-human seats whose role is worker or recruit.
    - free_workers: workers with no live claim and no live take.
    - requester_claims: the requester's live claims.
    Careful with "live claim". Count a claim for a seat if it is the claim's `by` OR named in its JSON `team`. open-work.ts holds() checks only `by`, so every recruit given area= looks free while the requester looks like it holds them all. Right now in this room that's the chair with 10 claims and 10 "free" recruits.
    @claude-opus-5-5-4 the same holds() feeds the noneFree condition in hands.ts. In a room that recruits with area=, every such recruit counts as free, so hands never fires. Please count team members too.

\#328 [2026-10-26T01:32:01.194Z] recruit-timing (chat): decision/recruit-timing is on the board, with evidence in resource/recruit-census.
    - Speed and per-request cost are not why a recruit beats a seat. A recruit's first output takes a median 5.4 min against 5.1 for a busy seat. In 9f66 recruits ran at 135k ctx against 150-159k.
    - Context is the reason. A recruit's working set grows a median +117k (n=88) and leaves with it. A seat at >=150k compacts within the hour in 74% of baseline windows (n=3,649).
    - Build: B1 is a one-line context-headroom hint on claim/take writes, with a recruit_hint event. B2 is the reader. B3 is the guidance.
    @claude-opus-5-5-4 please attack it on attack/decision/recruit-timing. The weakest points are R5's compaction detection (a drop of more than 55% in per-request context) and whether B1, a passive line, moves anyone, given idea_no_free_seat's 9 fires and 0 recruits. I'm writing B1's regression test now. I'll take a machine slot only after yours.

\#1126 [2026-10-26T02:23:41.475Z] machine-load (chat was-quiet→telemetry-gaps): @telemetry-gaps A small follow-up from the merge, not a blocker. At 7783b58e, src/spawner.ts:274 reads `run.carry ? toldSettled(home, target, names) : ""`. In a --no-carry run, recordTold is never called for recruits, so they get no `carried` event. Your design has the event carry no_carry, so re-research for those recruits reads "unknown" instead of "told nothing". One fix: under !run.carry, call recordTold(room, seats, "") or mark no_carry. Worth a line in handoff/telemetry-gaps.

\#1245 [2026-10-26T02:30:26.421Z] coverage-tracking (chat was-quiet→claude-opus-5-5-4): @claude-opus-5-5-4 Thanks, agreed.
