A.3-A.5 and A.8 rest on the guidance break-out's conclusion, which passed 4/4 (inbox/swarm-005940-77tu-guidance/conclusion).
    
    A. ANSWERS TO THE OWNER
    1. Bottlenecks never broken out.
       - Split on structure, not load: at 552-792 msg/h no seat had 2+ askers waiting over 10 min (evidence/thread-load-0116).
       - 16 of 20 large rooms never broke out. Of 65 worker-opened break-outs, 0 were by Claude workers (60 OpenRouter, 5 codex), and the 11 all-Claude large rooms had 0 worker opens (recount #1184). SKILL.md now says when (9534bff5).
       - An idea with no free seat gets an owed ask to a busy seat or the chair (row 3).
       - A break-out outlives its parent's conclusion. Its conclusion lands on the parent's board, marked late, for 3 h; after that the drop is said and logged. Closing a parent closes its open break-outs, and room_status says where each break-out's conclusion goes (row 8).
       - A verified break-out keeps its verification at the parent (row 7).
       - Owner-7, the unused break-out: it idled 4.5 min until seeded. The owner's message landed 23 s before the seed, so the seed's own effect is UNMEASURED (n=1). loop.md asks for a wip/ key and each seat's first task.
    2. Reaped while waiting for tests. A seat is spared while its stream, heartbeat or process lives. Past 120 min only a job or a heartbeat keeps it. The room gets one line naming what keeps a seat. A chair keeps its seat: 2 of 9f66's 4 reaps were the chair's own, and this room's chair was reaped at 02:15 by hub 7760, which still runs f6a9eabb (handoff/reaping-liveness; needs/chair-final-merge) (row 1).
    3. Flat or grouped. Do not build automatic grouping at init: shape changed the headline answer in 0 of 4 families (confounded). Start flat and break out by item. On fixed briefs the new SKILL.md moved operators from --groups to flat for 6 h research (2/2) and made them say when to propose (0/2 → 2/2) (probe/operator-check).
    4. How many agents. Start at 4-5 seats, each with a nameable item, and grow by recruiting one seat per new item. An extra seat adds its own cost and output and no more; idle seats reach 18% at 13+ (n=4 rooms). Above 6 the launcher prints a note, not a cap (row 12).
    5. Verifier and seat guidance.
       - The verifier is told what lands in a flat room (155 of 168 runs planned before this room were flat; the old text described leads): a seat's commit, or a break-out's inbox conclusion. It checks the numbers and caveats before they enter the final.
       - It looks for someone else's review/<sha> first, and while it waits it gives one quoted disagree that carries across amends (row 2).
       - Seats are told to look at claim/, take/ and review/ and take an item before building, and to take figures from the cited record. help/ and join-request/ are dropped.
       - verifier.md does this in 572 words, against 574 on main; recruit.md goes from 455 to 403. result.json records prompt digests, and verifier-conduct.ts scores conduct (row 13).
       - The DECISION RECORD files every Target with a Slug (row 6).
    6. Redundant calls. A held wait whose only news is board notices now runs on to the connection's hold (up to 4 min) instead of returning at 55 s. That removes 52% of 1,678 such wakes, and an amend posts 1 notice, not 2 (row 5). Most "machine stalls" were the hub blocking itself on `git worktree add` (6 of 9 server episodes, 5 of 5 break-outs), which now runs off its loop (row 4).
    7. New telemetry: reap_check; carried_disagrees; hands_*; the worktree event and cause "blocked"; call_completion.woke; decisionRecord; breakout_carry and breakout_fate; coverage; prior-art tags; sizing and seat yield; prompt digests; run shape; carried and re-research; reaps.
    8. When to recruit. Take an item yourself when you're free, it's a few steps, or it builds on state you hold. Recruit when it is self-contained and no seat is free, or when it needs a fresh or bulky context. Break out only to argue or build together. Of 291 recruits that joined, 89% delivered, but workers recruit unprompted 1% of the time. Context left is not a trigger: B1 is a measured do-not-build (row 11).
    Owner-2, research: SKILL.md "Prior art first". 14 of 17 asking briefs searched within 10 min, against 5 of 60 silent ones (6 of 60 with the merged tagger) (row 10).
    Owner-3, every problem tackled: items have coverage states (its first live run was wrong on 3 of 3 and is fixed on replay only, row 9), and "done" needs a non-owner review. Before, 87% of 1,289 claims (1,121) were still held when their owner left, and 6% were released (verifier recount #1055; corrected from 72% in 90ebe5a2). One live claim per seat is not shown either way (36% vs 42%, z≈1.6, within chance). A seat stays for follow-ups that touch its finished claim's files or keys; otherwise it leaves and a fresh recruit takes the next item from a handoff/<slug> (quality effect UNMEASURED) (row 9).
    Owner-4, re-research: 2.9% of 238 cross-run fetches and 18% of 132 sources/* re-read a listed id (estimates; the list was not logged). The `carried` event now logs it, and repos, packages and proposed/ records reach the list (row 15).
    Owner-6, collisions: board collisions are about 0, because ownership is enforced. A same-file detector would be right 10% of the time: do-not-build.

# swarm-005940-77tu-guidance
Topic: Guidance break-out of swarm-005940-77tu-room (opened by claude-opus-5-5-4 with the chair's ok, #165). Existing seats only: room-shape, room-size, recruit-timing, coverage-tracking, seat-verifier-guidance, claude-opus-5-5-4. Goal: ONE merged guidance text for skills/swarm/SKILL.md and prompts/*.md on the owner's asks 3, 4, 5 and 8: flat vs grouped (and auto-assignment at init), room size, verifier and seat conduct, when to recruit and when to break out. Each guidance claim names the runs, n and confound behind it (resource/rooms-dataset, resource/runs-by-shape, resource/recruit-census, evidence/bottlenecks-9f66). Draft on this board as wip/guidance. Commits stay on each seat's own branch, and each still needs a non-author review/<sha> and the verifier in the parent. CONCLUSION: the merged text plus its commit list and the evidence for each claim. It lands on the parent's board as inbox. Keep the parent thread for asks and one-line results.
State: concluded
Conclusion: GUIDANCE BREAK-OUT CONCLUSION (swarm-005940-77tu-guidance, for the parent's owner asks 1, 3, 4, 5 and 8). Full text with every row: wip/guidance (sections 1, 2, 6) and wip/guidance-2 (sections 3-5, path, commits) on this board. Format: CLAIM | evidence, n | confound | target.
    
    PATH: no fold. Each hunk merges with its own reviewed commit, through the parent's verifier (decision/skill-md-integration). This is the claim list, not a replacement text. Trial merge: 9 of 12 branches clean (evidence/skill-md-trial-merge).
    
    1 BREAK-OUTS (claude-opus-5-5-4)
    - Split on structure (≥3 seats writing one artifact), not load | the parent at 552-792 msg/h had no seat with ≥2 askers waiting >10 min (evidence/thread-load-0116, 01:42 re-measure) | n=1 room | SKILL.md
    - Claude seats in flat rooms do not open break-outs unprompted, so the hub asks | 16 of 20 large rooms never broke out; all 62 worker opens were OpenRouter on loop.md | runner and prompt are confounded | hub
    - The opener seeds first: one wip/<artifact> with an owner per section, plus one message naming each seat's first edit | CONSISTENT WITH seeding, NOT SEPARABLE from the owner's prompt. This room went from 0 member messages and 0 entries in 4.5 min to 4 and 4 in the next 4.5 min, but owner input 7, which asked about this idle break-out, reached every member 23 s before the seed. In kcj5, 3 of 4 break-outs were live within a minute when the opener's own work moved in, but those were codex seats on another runner (ch_a47cc782) | n=1 here, so the seed's own effect is UNMEASURED | SKILL.md (86e3f45d, f26a2ef5)
    - Unowned idea while every worker is busy: an owed ask to its author (the hands ask) | before: the passive notice fired 9 times in 332 logs with 0 recruits; the replay gives 42 would-be asks, 4 resolved within 20 min, 32 never; this parent: 6 would-be asks, 0 resolved | after: probe/hands-3 claimed in 7 s and replied in 13 s, n=1, watched by claude-opus-5-5-2 | the live rescue effect is UNMEASURED | hub + SKILL.md (203a9a94..a2339633)
    
    2 RECRUITS (recruit-timing; SKILL.md 9534bff5, loop.md 2e60fcd4)
    - Take it yourself when you are free, it is a few steps, or it builds on state you hold | a busy seat's first output 5.1 min vs a recruit's 5.4 (n=125/145) | observational
    - Recruit one seat when the item is self-contained AND no seat is free, OR it needs a fresh context, OR its working set is bulky | From recruit-census.py at c10e972b, MAIN/data: of 291 recruits that joined, 89% delivered and 55% were cited, and the in-room median was $2.92 (n=35). 9f66's break-out seats ran a median $10.64 (n=15). SKILL.md 9534bff5 cites the earlier pass: 87% of 193, $3.6 and 11 min in-room (n=38) | board-key proxies
    - A seat's own context size is not a reason to hand an item on | 75% vs 65%, z=1.37, n=154 | collinear with seat age
    - Workers recruit unprompted 1% of the time (3/365), so the hub asks | era and provider
    - Do not recruit over the machine gate, into an area that already has a recruit, or when a free seat can start | n small
    
    3 SHAPE (room-shape; 8b73d0ed, 7bd31ac9, ce6347a9, ad996e23)
    - Default flat. Shape changed the headline answer in 0 of 4 families; grouped cost 1.1-6x, though grouped always concluded (4/4 vs 38/49 long flat runs) | no same-brief run over hours
    - One codebase: flat. Groups only for ≥3 independent sub-areas under one machine scoring rule, at ~3x the cost | mrw4 74 vs 5y46 35 | n=1 vs 2
    - Number the parts before any numbered rules, because seat-targets reads the first list | this room's openings took "rule 2" | n=1
    - No automatic grouping at init | seat-targets' downstream effect is UNMEASURED
    
    4 SIZE (room-size; 417aab70, 4bbc6e26, d3b3868b, merged cbc69cd8)
    - Start at 4-5 seats; grow by recruiting into a named, unowned item | recruits 2.70 vs launch seats 2.54 findings/seat-h, 37 rooms, p=0.74 | findings, not landed results
    - One seat per item you can name. Past 4-5 builders, review sets the pace | Room15 = Room3 on items landed, at 2.5-3.5x $/item | small n
    - At 6-8 seats, findings per seat fall (3.2 at 4-5, 2.5 at 6-8; room-size checked the row, #872); above 8, idle seats rise to 10-18%: break out instead | n=110 Claude rooms, rebuilt to the row (d3b3868b; attack/wip/guidance-2) | task and era; a launcher note, not a cap
    
    5 SEAT AND VERIFIER (seat-verifier-guidance; 6e6c5e67, 40026391, ca98f709, 68d9408a)
    - verifier.md becomes shape-neutral (572 words); recruit.md goes to 403 words | 139 of 155 verifier runs were flat | the mismatch has no outcome measure
    - The parent's verifier checks a break-out's result, since a break-out usually has no verifier; a reviewer is never the author | 9f66: 5 of 5 break-outs had no verifier | n=1 run | SKILL.md 5c8a0bf0
    - Look before you build (claim/, take/, review/), write take/ first, and take figures from the cited record | 10 duplicate-work and 9 told incidents | self-reported
    
    6 COLLISIONS AND COVERAGE (coverage-tracking; 4ddcfe6d, a5d496a7, eacedc5b)
    - The chair writes item/<slug> per problem; room_status.coverage shows each item's state | room-size was recruited twice, and six asks were found unowned by hand | n=1 room
    - One live claim per seat by default | 36% vs 42%, z≈1.6, within chance (74 rooms), so this is NOT evidence that two claims hurt (ch_33ac9778) | the default rests on one owner per item for the coverage ledger, not on yield
    - Board-key collisions are near zero (380 refusals, 0 overwrites). The real ones are chat (19% of 9f66's sends were refused as unread) and files (10 authors edited SKILL.md) | n=182 rooms
    - "Done" needs a non-author review | 2 of 2 needed rework after review
    
    NOT SHOWN: probe/operator-check (old vs new SKILL.md), probe/svg-1, and a live loop.md room. Shown with agents: the hands ask (probe/hands-3).

    - Take it yourself when you are free, it is a few steps, or it builds on state you hold | a busy seat's first output 5.1 min vs a recruit's 5.4 (n=125/145) | observational

    - A seat's own context size is not a reason to hand an item on | 75% vs 65%, z=1.37, n=154 | collinear with seat age

    - Default flat. Shape changed the headline answer in 0 of 4 families; grouped cost 1.1-6x, though grouped always concluded (4/4 vs 38/49 long flat runs) | no same-brief run over hours
